81b6(rgyud): na med pa zung //pad+ma karma me dang chongs//rin chen bla med gsal bar gyis//dkyil 'khor tshul du spros nas kyang //mchod rten tshul du bsdu bar gyis//lha klu gnod sbyin dri za dang //lha min mkha' lding mi'am ci//srin po sha za brjed byed tshogs//'jigs pa dag 
81b7(rgyud): kyang 'jigs 'gyur ba'i//rnam 'phrul du ma 'di sgyur cig/gsung mchog de skad thos ma thag/lag na rdo rje mthu bo ches//dga' mgu thal mo rab sbyar te//snyan pa'i tshig gis 'di gsol to//gser dang rin chen lnga po las//rab mchog ri bong gyur 
82a1(rgyud): @#//pa las//'gro rnams kun gyi rten gnas dang //rtag pa bu yang dag pa don pa ltar//rnam par shes dang phung po bzhi//'byung ba rnam par dag bsgrubs pas//lha dang phyag rgya phrin las su//sngags shes rnams kyis bzung byas nas//brtul ba'i ye shes 
82a2(rgyud): byang chub rim//khyad par de la ldan par 'gyur//de nas bcom ldan grub pa che//kun mkhyen ye shes gtso bo ches//sku las sna tshogs mdangs 'od can//'od zer du ma byung nas kyang //gzhi dang snying po me tog gis//brgyan pa'i zhing rnams khyab byas tshe//
82a3(rgyud): sa yang rnam pa drug tu g.yos//'jig rten spang dang bdud kyi tshogs//ma lus pa rnams brgyal bar 'gyur//nyan thos rang rgyal 'dar bar 'gyur//byang chub sems dpa' blo ldan gyi//tshogs rnams shin tu skrag par gyur//de tshe phyogs bcu'i zhing rnams nas//
82a4(rgyud): sangs rgyas kun gyi mdzod spu nas//khro bo chen po'i tshogs byung ba//'od zer rtse mos drangs nas su//zhing dbus dkyil 'khor 'di ru lhags//'od zer tshogs kyang rab 'dus nas//dpal be'u nyid du thim par gyur//de tshe 'od de'i byin brlabs las//
82a5(rgyud): mkha' la sgra 'di grag par gyur//shAkya'i mgon po rdzogs sangs rgyas//gang du gzi brjid che bzhugs slad//chos kyi dbyings las byung ba yi//rgyal ba'i sras po thams cad gshegs//dri ma med pa'i dam chos mchog/gsang sngags spyod pa'i 
82a6(rgyud): tshul dge ba//thos nas byang chub 'dus ma byas//'jig rten mgon po brnyes par gyur//de nas lag na rdo rje tshogs//'khor gyi dkyil 'khor thams cad kyang //snyim pa me tog bkang byas te//gtor nas phyag 'tshal bskor ba dang //dbang 
82a7(rgyud): zhing gus pa'i tshul gyis su//bus btsugs thal sbyar gsol ba 'debs//mi gnas ye shes che ba'i mchog/yon tan rgya mtsho 'byung ba'i gtso//mtshan nyid thams cad rab brgyan pa//mtshan min spangs pa'i thub pa che//dri med dge ba ji ltar lags//gsang 
82b1(rgyud): sngags chul du spyod pa ci//'dus ma byas pa'i byang chub gang //'jig rten mgon po ci ltar 'gyur//bdag cag rnams la sman rab dang //'gro la thugs rje che mdzad phyir//mgon po bcom ldan thub pa ches//yang dang yang du bshad du gsol//de nas yang 
82b2(rgyud): dag rdzogs sangs rgyas//rkang gnyis gtso bos bka' stsal pa//gsang ba'i dbang phyug rdo rje 'dzin//shes rab chen po'i spyan ldan pas//bdag gzhan phan don la brtson phyir//legs par nyon la yid la zung //yang dag yang dag mtha' min tshul//
82b3(rgyud): de ni de yis bshad par bya//srog gcod ma byin len brdzun 'khrig/tshig rtsub dbyen dang dag kul smra//brnab sems gnod sems log lta chang //dri do ngan pa'i zas skom dang //srog can sha khrag log 'tshor bcas//slob dpon dge 'dun mchod rten sogs//
82b4(rgyud): spyod par yul can yo byad dang //dman zhing nyam thag srog dgu pa//glegs bam 'tshong dang chos kyi zong //g.yang sa ral gri so dug sogs//me 'obs ltar mthong spangs nas kyang //rnam par dkar la sogs par bya//yi ge 'dri mchod sbyin stsol dang //
82b5(rgyud): rgan dang 'dzin dang klog pa dang //mchod pa dang ni kha ton dang //de dag sems dang sgom pa ste//dkar ba du lam la yun ring dang //nus bcas rgyun mi 'chad du 'bad//gtan pa med pa'i byang chub sems//shel ltar dri ma med pa'o//mchog 'di spyad pas sdug 
82b6(rgyud): bsngal tshogs//myur du gcod pas dge ba'o//chos bdag med dang mnyam nyid dang //rang sems gdod nas dag pa gang //rgyu 'bras 'du byed med pa dang //zag bcas bde dang bral ba las//byang chub phyogs chos ma lus pa//chos nyid bdag med spyod pa 
82b7(rgyud): ni//chos dbyings ye shes mngon byas nas//ma 'dres chos rnams rdzogs gsal ba//'dus ma byas kyi byang chub ste//chos rnams kun gyi gzhi lus so//'dod pa'i khams sogs yon tan la//shin tu bde rol spyad pa spyod//sangs rgyas mu stegs 
83a1(rgyud): @#//can rnams kyi//glog tu skur ba gsang ba ste//bskal pa bsam yas dpag med du//thams cad dpal 'dra ba rgyal rgya che/rnams//ljags dang phyag rgya ting 'dzin du//lus ngag yid la gang gnas pa//dbyer med tshul du skyob pas na//sangs rgyas kun 
83a2(rgyud): gyis sngags su brjod//mtshan dang dpe byad ldan pa yis//yon tan mtha' dag rdzogs pa ni//bden pa gnyis la gnas pa yi//byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi mgon//la la 'khor los ldan pa'i gzugs//la la rang gi sbyong chen po'i gzugs//la lar dran 
83a3(rgyud): po srin mo'i gzugs//la lar 'gro ba bza' mo'i gzugs//chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba dang //seng ge ltar gzigs mdzad la sogs//sna tshogs du ma mdzad pa ni//gang gnyis lha min rnam gnyis sogs//'gro ba'i mgon por gyur pa'o//rtag dang 'dra dang des 
83a4(rgyud): med pa'i//gtso bo bsam pas gyur pa rnams//'jig rten phal pa'i mgon po ste//gzugs byad kha dog tha dad pa//yul dus rnam pa'i bye brag gis//grangs ma byung ba mchod 'os so//thub pas gsungs pa'i de ma thag/rdo rje can gyis bzhengs nas kyang //
83a5(rgyud): phyag 'tshal bskor ba byas nas ni//spyan sngar 'dug nas 'di skad gsol//'dren pa gtso bo bcom ldan 'das//sngags zhes pa ni gang gang yin//phyag rgya zhes ni ci lta bu//ting 'dzin zhes ni ji ltar bshad//lus ngag yid la ji ltar gnas//dbyer med 
83a6(rgyud): tshul du ji ltar skyob//bdag la phan pa'i don phyir dang //gzhan la brtse phyir bshad du gsol//de nas rgyal ba gtso mchog ni//ljags kyi dbang po bkram byas te//tshangs pa chen po'i gsung snyan gyis//rnam rtog zad phyir 'di gsungs so//snying po nye 
83a7(rgyud): snying rtsa ba'i sngags//las kyi sngags su gyur pa ste//snying po brjod dang sa bon no//nye snying ye shes mchod dang ni//gsang ba'i snying po zhes grags so//las kyi sngags ni gnyis yin te//bdag nyid brtan byed las sngags dang //sbyor tshogs grub par byed 
83b1(rgyud): pa'o//phyag rgya lag pa'i 'du byed dang //rkang pa stabs kyi 'gyur ba'i rtogs pa su mtshon dang bde bas pa dang //'gyur ba med par byed pas na//phyag rgya zhes su bshad pa'o//rang gi rtse gcig sems bsdus na//sems la lhag pa'i chos 'bebs pa//
83b2(rgyud): de ni ting nge 'dzin zhes bya//de ltar de dag la brten nas//snga ma'i gnas de yongs gyur nas//sgo nga bral ba'i 'dab ma blangs ltar//lus 'di mkha' la cher song nas//rgyal ba'i zhing khams thams cad du//rgyal la dam chos mnyan nas kyang //rgyal sras 
83b3(rgyud): kun la bshad pa dang //skad cig mkhyen pa'i shes pas kyang //shes bya ma lus phyogs khyab pa//lus ngag yid la gnas pa'o//sems dang yid ni 'dres pa'i tshe//ye shes bde bar shar ba ni//dus gsum sangs rgyas thams cad kyi//dbyer med 
83b4(rgyud): tshul du skyob ces gsungs//'phags pa rdo rje khro bo'i rgyal po'i rtog pa bsdus pa'i rgyud las lhag par gnas pa'i don yongs su bkod pa'i le'u ste dang po'o////zhi bas gdul dka' bag gis tshad dang //log 'dren de rnams 'dug pa'i phyir//
83b5(rgyud): sems kyi bye brag thugs chud nas//de dang gzugs 'dras gzhom pa'i phyir//rtog med nam mkha'i lam gnas te//rdzogs sangs gdul dka' 'dul mdzad sku//rab 'phags khro bo stobs chen dpal//sangs rgyas kun gyi sras kyi sras//mi g.yo'i 
83b6(rgyud): mtshan grags de la 'dir//rgyal dang rgyal sras rtag phyag 'tshal//tshangs sogs lha dang lus can kun//de la rtag tu mchod par byed//rgyal bas byas gzo rgyal sras kyi//smon rdzogs bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i//ye shes rdzogs nas mi g.yo'i sa//
83b7(rgyud): thog mas mi g.yo ba zhes bya//phas kyi rgol 'joms dpa' bo ste//tshar gcod pa ni khro bo yin//sngags kyi bden tshig mi slu bas//nyan thos bdud tshogs kyis mi g.yo//rtog med bdud la khro bas rgyal//rdzu 'phrul bskyod med mi g.yo ba//de 
84a1(rgyud): @#//bzhin nyid kyi snying po mchog/stong nyid las byung rgyal ba'i gtsos dbyings bdag nyid la bzhugs nas//sprul 'dis srid pa'i khams sbyong mdzad//mkha' ltar dri med spros bral bas//dus gsum sangs rgyas thams cad 'byung //spros med nam mkha'i 
84a2(rgyud): rang bzhin la//tha snyad tsam du gsang sngags zhes//brjod kyang rag gi spyod yul 'das//skyob pas tha snyad rtogs phyir gsungs//sems med rang bzhin med stong nyid//sgyu 'drar yid kyis bsgom pa yin//dngos med mnyam mdzes 'dod don rnams//gsang sngags 
84a3(rgyud): mgon 'di brjod pas stsal//mu stegs bdag dang klog gang zag/gtso bo skyes bu khyab gnas pas//'khor ba rtag 'dod nga rgyal rmongs//log pa'i zhags bcings sdug bsngal bskyed//bdag med srog med sems can med//byed po med cing gtso bo med//khyab 
84a4(rgyud): gnas med cing dngos po med//rdzas la rdzogs pa'i rang bzhin med//log rtog srid 'ching zhags bcad phyir//dngos med ma tshogs gzugs bsgoms pas//dngos grol stong nyid go 'phang 'thob//phyi nang khyad par der goms pa'o//dbu bcu bur sgyu 'drar bsgom//
84a5(rgyud): stong nyid las ldang ting 'dzin gyis//rnam rtog sdig bcings las grol nas//'chi med ye shes go 'phang 'thob//phung po khams dang skye mched med//kun gzhi med de gang zag med//gzung 'dzin med de bcings med par//rgyal ba rnams kyis rtag tu bshad//
84a6(rgyud): kun med kun bral kun spangs pa'i//stong nyid byang sems sgyu 'dra bar//bag yod pas ni rtag bsgoms na//byang chub sems kyi myu gu 'char//yid bzhin nor 'dra rdzu 'phrul che//mkha' ltar gos pa med pa'i sku//sngags spyod byang chub sems dpa' 
84a7(rgyud): yis//byang chub sems kyi sgom pa 'thob//ngo mtshar gsungs te thos ma thag/khro bo rnams kyi gtso la bstod//rtag tu ngag cing dri med pa//rnam rtog rgyun 'gags kun rtog med//brtag rtog med la zhugs pa yi//dpa' bo chen po phyag 'tshal bstod//chos kun g.yo 
84b1(rgyud): pa med pa khyod//spyod yul kun tu legs sbyar bas//kun mkhyen g.yo ba med pa rtag/rang bzhin blos bral khyod phyag 'tshal//sems can sna tshogs phan don du//mdzad spyod sna tshogs gsungs pa'o//dag pa'i lam rnams yang dag 'byung //rnam dag sems la 
84b2(rgyud): phyag 'tshal bstod//mun pa phra rab rdul spangs pa'i//sprin bral nam mkha' dag pa ltar//mgon po khyod sku shel sgong ltar//kun rtog spangs la phyag 'tshal bstod//spros med nam mkha' lta bu la//dngos po thams cad dang bral bas//phung khams skye mched 
84b3(rgyud): shin tu dag/ye shes rgya mtsho phyag 'tshal bstod//mkha' la 'ja' shar sna tshogs ltar//gtso khyod dag pa'i sems can gzigs//ngan 'gro kun sgrol dpa' chen 'khrungs//go cha chen po phyag 'tshal bstod//chos nyid las byung snying po ni//yi ge gcig pa gzi brjid 
84b4(rgyud): che//sems can sdug bsngal sa gzhi la//mun 'khrigs sel la phyag 'tshal bstod//sngags mchog gtso bo'i gsang ba 'dis//rgyal sras chos 'phel shes rab bskyed//bla med go 'phang myur 'gro gsungs//gtso bo khyod la phyag 'tshal bstod//dngos kun myur dang bcas 
84b5(rgyud): pa yi//las dang skye las rnam par grol//rgyal ba rgyu med bdag nyid 'khrungs//gtso mchog khyod la phyag 'tshal bstod //dngos med ye shes me lce yis//'dus byas ri bo kun bsregs nas//chos dbyings rang bzhin gcig tu rgyal//bla med sku la phyag 'tshal bstod//
84b6(rgyud): bstod pa'i don de thugs chud nas//'gro la phan phyir cho ga gsungs//gsang sngags log par spyod pa dang //ser sna log par goms pa las//'dod pas non pa'i sems rtog gis//sngags pa yi dgas gnas su skye//dang pos mar me me tog dang //zhal zas 
84b7(rgyud): gtor ma phreng ba sogs//mchod pa'i rdzas dang yo byad dang //mi yi lus dang kha zas la//'tshe zhing sgrub pa'i bar du gcod//mi sdug nad bskyed lo tog 'tshe//gnyis pa gnod sbyin drag gyur nas//sha khrag chang la snom zhing rgyug/thos bsam 
85a1(rgyud): @#//bsgrub pa'i bar gcod cing //nad bskyed tshe len log lta bskyed//rig sngags 'chang gi gdon gzhom phyir//sgrub pos 'bad de sngags brjod bya//sngags mthu bskyed pa gtor ma dang //sbyin sreg phyag rgya sngags bcas dang //mnan pa'i las kyis 
85a2(rgyud): nags me ltar//thogs pa med par 'joms par 'gyur//de dang brtan byed lha yin te//de gsal byed pa ras ris yin//de kun rnam dag gsol byed pa//byang chub sems mchog zhes ni//log rtog 'khor ba'i zi chun la//ral gri rnon po'i sta re ltar//
85a3(rgyud): 'phags pa rdo rje khro bo'i rgyal po'i rtog pa bsdus pa'i rgyud las de bzhon nyid kyi le'u ste gnyis pa'o////de nas sngags dang phyag rgya dag/'byung bar rab tu bshad par bya//na maHma nas pa ri pUng ki b+h+yaHsarba ta thA ga te b+h+yaHsarba thA hUM/snying po dam pa'o//na maHsangs pa 
85a4(rgyud): ri pU ra ke b+h+yaHsarba ta thA ga te byA ka sarba dA tsit+ta ma hA so la tsa soT ya hUM/sa ta he maM lIng ba'o//ra maHsangs pa ri pU ra ke b+h+yaHsarbata thA ga te b+h+yaHsarba ta thA ha ta a mo b+ha/tsaN+Da ma hA ro Sha Na soT ya hUM/tra ma ya h+yaM/Turba he maM nye ba'i snying po'o//na maHsarba pa ri pU ra ke b+h+ya/
85a5(rgyud): sarba ta thA ga te b+h+ya/sarba nA thA a tsa yA Na na/nA na nA/mo nA mo nA/sa tA saT+Ta/ta ka ta Ta/tiSh+Tha tiSh+Tha/A be sha ya ta/ma ya tha/a ya tha/ta Ni ta Ni/ka da ya Na ya/bi kAn/ma ra ma ra da Sha ka/rak+Sha rak+Sha sa ma rA du ku ru/ki ri ki ri/ma na bi dza ya ba dza ya thA hUM hUM/tri bi la ta ra 
85a6(rgyud): s+ya/na ta ka ta hi hi/a tsa la/ti tse/ya pha ka/a hUM/a sa man ka/ma tha/ma hA ba ya sa da ma na ya/tra ma hAM/sha ru du po ka tu Sh+yan tu yo ka ba dza na maHsA pra ta ta ba ye h+yo/dza la ma tra ta/ya sa ta na maHsa tA/gsang ba'i snying po'o//tA nas phyag rgya ltar lta bas kyi//
85a7(rgyud): sngags brjod pa ni 'dir gsungs so//na maHsa na sa pa ri pU ra ci thugs ma ta thA ga te hu ka sarba thA/o ha na ha na ma ra nA ra ya dza na//ma ra nA pa ra/ra ma gro'i phyag tu 'jigs so//na ma sa na sa pa ri pU rake b+h+ya ka sarba ta thA na te b+h+ya/sarba thA/o kr-i ta gr-i ta//bA da khA da/
85b1(rgyud): rnam shan|ban+d+ha ban+d+h+ya/ma hA ar+ha te/d+harma te mA dA/zhags pa'i'o//nam mahA sa pa ri pU ra ke b+h+yaH/sarba ta thA ga te b+h+yaH/sarba thA/oM bak+Sha rak+Sha/ha na ha Ta/kuT+Ta kuT+Ta/mA ra ya bud+d+ha yi nu ma ke hUM dza/ga Ni'i'o//na maHsa nA sa pa ri pU ra kai b+h+yaH/sarba ta thA ga ti thA/sarba thA/
85b2(rgyud): oM d+harma d+ha ra badzra na yi/kha T+wAM ga dA sha d+ha ra swA hA/gtsug tor gyi'o//na maHsa nA sa ba ri pU ra ke b+h+yaH/sarba ta thA ga te b+h+yaHsarba thA/oM b+ha Sha b+ha da ha pa tsa b+ha sme ku ru ku ru ha na ha na badzra/br-i da b+ha pr-i ha da ra/tho ba'i'o//na maHsa nA sa ma raM pU ra ke b+h+yaH/sa rba ta thA ga te b+ha Sha /
85b3(rgyud): sarba thA/oM Sh+Tha na ta mu ti/da ha da ha/sA d+ha ya sA d+ha ya/bi dza ya bi dza ya/bi dza ya kI ti swA hA/mgo bo'i phyag rgya'o//na maHsa nA sa paraM pU ra ke b+h+yaH/sarbata thA ga te b+h+yaH/sarbA shA/oM b+hu d+ha ra d+ha ra/a tsi na dru pa la bi kra ma NaM/pur pu sa la ya/rak+Sha rak+Sha sarba mA hA/
85b4(rgyud): rdo ba'i phyag rgya'o//na maHsarbA sa pa ri pU ra ke b+h+yaH/sarba ta thA ga ti b+h+yaH/sarba thA/oM badzra gr-i da ya/badzra ma taM gi swA hA/snying ga'i phyag rgya'o//na maHsadzA sa pa ri pU ra ke/b+h+yaHsarba ta thA ga te b+h+yaH/sarba thA/oMhUM da ha da ga ki ni tsi rA la si/gA na ya shI g+h+raM/rak+Sha rak+Sha/
85b5(rgyud): ban+d+ha ban+d+ha/duSh+TaM/dam dam swA hA/phyogs bcing ba'o//na maHsarbA sa ba ri pU ra ke b+h+yaH/sarba ta thA ga te b+h+ya sarba thA/oM du na du na ma hA sA sha kha D+ga dA rA sa pa ri pU ra keswA hA/bka' rtags kyi'o//na maHsa nA sa pa ri pU ru ke b+h+yaHsarba ta thA ga te b+hA sarba thA/oM ga ga na gan+d+ho 
85b6(rgyud): d+ha ba/mahA sa ma ya/pa ri pU ra ke swA hA/dri'i phyag rgya'o//na maHsargA sa pa ri pU ra keb+h+yaH/sarba ta thA ga te b+h+yaHsarba thA/oM bi shud+d+ha dz+nyA na rI dzas b+ha be ga ga na nAM dAHswA hA/ma mo'i phyag rgya'o//namaHsarbA sa pa ri pU ra ke b+h+yaHsarba ta thA gte b+h+yaHsarba thA/oMshwad d+he 
85b7(rgyud): d+he ma te/shud+d+ha shud+d+ha/gr-iha gr-i h+ya/sa dA sa pa ri pU ra ki b+h+yaHswA hA/gtong ma'i phyag rgya'o//na maHa nA sa pa ri pU ra ked+haH/sarba ta thA ga te bU na sarba thA/oM shu d+ha ma hI dz+nyA na swA hA de/tiSh+Tha tiSh+Tha/ma hA na ye swA hA/bdug pa'i yo//na maHsa bAs pa ri pU ra ked+d+hA/sarba 
86a1(rgyud): @#//ta thA ga te b+h+yaH/sarba thA/oM ga ga na bi krAn+ta gA ma ni ma hA shu d+hA na yi swA hA/me tog gi'o//na maHsa nA sa pa ri pU ra ki b+h+yaH/sarba ta thA ga te b+h+yaH/sarbaHthA/oM ha na ha na/bar ma bi shud+d+he d+harma na ya pu ri pU ra ke b+h+yaHswA hA/zhal zas kyi'o//na maHsa nA sa ba ri pU ra ke b+h+yaH/sarba thA/sarba thA/oM a pra ti hi ho na d+ya ta/da ma da ma/sarba thA/sa pa ri pU ra ke b+h+yaHshA hA/dpa' bo kun drang ba'i ngo//na maHsa nA sa bA ri pU ra ke b+h+yaH/sarba ta thA ga te b+h+yaH/sarba ha nA/oM bi shud+d+ha bi shud+d+ha/sa kra sa b+hA bi yA na ya/ma hA na le gA dA/
86a2(rgyud): byang chub sems dpa' spyan drang ba'o//na maHsarbA sa pa ri pU ra ke b+h+ya ཿ/sarba ta thA ga te byaH/sarba thA/oM bi shud+d+hA gA na/ha na ha ma/da ma da ma/ma dA na ye swA hA/nyan thos spyan drang ba'i ngo//na ma sa nA sa pa ri pU ra keb+h+yaH/sarba ta thA ga te b+h+yaHsarba thA/oM ba hA/ke rA ya/
86a3(rgyud): shI k+ShAM/da ha da ha/pa tsa ba tsa/ma hA pa ri sA ra swA hA/pho nya pho mo spyan drang ba'o//na maHsarbA sa pa ri pUr ke b+h+yaH/sarba ta thA ga ta b+h+yaH/sarba thA/oM ma thA ma thA/A na ya A na ya/pra bi sha ya/pra be sha ya/sarba ta thA ga ta ga rba de be ta ma ta ma/ma hA na ya ba ri pA ri hUM swA hA/las 
86a4(rgyud): rten pa rnams spyan drang ba'o//na maHma nA sa pa ri pU rake b+h+yaHsarba ta thA ga te b+h+yaH/sarba thA/oMs sa h+ya sa t+ya/ma hA sA d+h+ya ya/da ma da ma/ma hA da ma/A kar+Sha ya A kar+Sha ya/a d+ye ba ma hA na ya shu d+he swA hA/drang srong spyan drang ba'o//na ma sarbA sa pa ri pU ra ke b+h+yaH/sarba ta tha ga te b+h+yaH/
86a5(rgyud): sarba thA/oM dza ya dza ya/bi dza ya bi dza ya/ma hA sa t+ya ba di ni/sarba h+l-i te swA hA/sring mo rnams spyan drang ba'o//ni maHsarbA sa pa ri pU rakai b+h+yaH/sarba ta thA ga te b+h+yaH/sarba thA/oM hrI hA dA/ma tha ma tha/da ma da ma/ha na ha na/ma hA na ye/ma ra ma ra/ha swA hA/ma mo rnams 
86a6(rgyud): spyan drang ba'o//na maHsarbA sa pa ri pUr ke b+h+yaH/sarba ta thA gte b+h+yaH/sarba thA/o ha na badzra hUM phaT/sarba duSh+Ta gra haM/ma tha ma tha/b+hany+dza b+hany+dza/sarba big+h+naM/da ma da ma/sarba duSh+TAM oM hUM phaT swA hA/rkang stabs tsham ngam gyi sngags so//na maHsa rA sa pa ri pU ra keb+h+yaH/
86b1(rgyud): sUrba ta thA ga te b+h+yaH/sarba thA/oM AHhUM/A kA sha ga ga na s+pha na na saM swA hA//'o thug khur ba 'bru sna tshogs//spros dang me tog la sogs pa//ci 'byor 'byor ba'i gtor ma ni//sngags des spro na nge 'brel dar bya//na maHsarba sa pa ri pU ra ki b+h+yaH/sarba ta thA ga te b+h+yaH/a pa thA/
86b2(rgyud): oM swa b+hA pa sarba thA b+h+yo ShAM swA hA/na maHsarbA sa pa ri pU ra ke d+h+yaH/sarba ta thA ga te g+h+yaH/sarba thA/oM la b+h+ye sarba ga ga na la b+h+yA ya swA hA/na maHsa rA sa pa ri pU ra ki d+h+yaHsarba ta thA ga te g+h+yaHsarba thA/oM sarba sid+d+hi dA d+ha mai ho swA hA/byin brlabs bzhin du bsgyur ba dang /kun gyis thim par 
86b3(rgyud): bya ba dang //'dod pa'i las rnams gang dang gang //grub par bya ba'i sngags yin no//gza' chen brgyad dang rgyu skar dang /nyi shu rtsa brgyad rnams kyi sngags//na maHsarbA sa pa ri bU ra ke b+h+yaH/sarba ta thA ga ta b+h+yaH/sarba thA/A di d+ya/so ma/aM kA raHpu d+ha tha/pr-i ha swa ti/
86b4(rgyud): shu graH/sha nai sh+tsa raH/rA tsaH/ke tuH/kr-i d+hi kA/rai dI tsI/mr-i ga shi rA/a ra da pu na tha su/pu Sha/a shri Sha/ma b+hA pu rbA pa shU nI u d+ha ra phalpU NAM/ha hA/tsa dA swa sti/bi shwa ShA/sa nu ra d+ha/dz+yoSh+Tha mU la/pU trA thA/u t+ta rA thA/pra NA a b+hi dzaM ta/sha ta b+hi ShA/d+ha ni rA/
86b5(rgyud): sUrba b+ha dra ba dA/u t+ta ra b+ha drA/r-i ba tA+e/a shwa nAM/b+ha ra nI/oM dang swA hA ra bcas pa ni//rang 'dra snying po'i sngags yin te//gang 'dod pas kyang sbyar bar bya//mchod pa'i rigs kyang tha dad bya/na maHsa man+ta kA ya bA k+tsit+ta badzrA nAM/oM a tsa ya ka Na 
86b6(rgyud): tsa d+ha ma hA ro Sha na s+pho Ta ya tra ka kha haM maM/na maHsa man+ta kA ya dA krit+ta badzrA nAM/oM badzra ka pa tsa du baM/oM na maHsarba ta thA ga te b+h+yaH/sarba b+ha ya bi ga te b+h+yaH/bi shwa mu khe b+h+yaH/sarba thA khaM ra kA/ma hA pa li/sarba ta thA ga te puN+ye ni rdzi te hUM hUM tra thA a pra ti ha te swA hA/khro bo spyan 
86b7(rgyud): gcig drag shul can//srung dang bcas par mchod pa dang //sngags bzlas dngos grub blangs pas 'grub//ci 'dod las la cho gas sbyar//lag g.yas gung mdzub gshibs te brkyang //srin lag mthe chung mthe bos mnan//shugs drangs tshul phyung 
87a1(rgyud): @#//mkha' la 'phyar//ral gri phyag rgyas bdud tshogs 'joms//g.yon pa khu tshur sdigs mdzub bsgreng //slar bsgyur byed bzhags pa'i rgya//gung mo mthe'u chung so sor sprad//mdzub mo ming med nang du dgug/mthe bo gshibs pa gung 'gram sbyar//snying 
87a2(rgyud): ga bkod de go cha'i rgyan//sor 'khyud khu tshur nang du bsdam//mtheb gnyis cung bsgyur mgo thod rgya//g.yon pa'i khu tshur mthe bo ni//g.yas pa'i khu tshur nang bcug nas//mthe bong g.yas bsgreng tho ba'i rgya//phyogs bcur brdzungs ba ltar bya'o//g.yas pa khu tshur gung mo 
87a3(rgyud): bsgreng //spyi gtsug phyag rgya gtsug tu bzhag/g.yon pa khu tshur gung mdzub bsgreng //bka' rtags phyag rgya rtag tu phyar//khu tshur gnyis gshibs lto sbyar ba//rdo ba lta bu'i phyag rgya'o//g.yas pa'i khu tshur snying gar bzhag/snying ga rgyan pa'i phyag rgya'o//
87a4(rgyud): lag gnyis sor mo so sor brkyang //phyogs bcu bcing ba'i phyag rgya'o//lag sbyar gung gnyer nang bcug pa//dri yi phyag byar bshad pa'o//khu tshur rgya ba sprad gung mthe bong //mthe bong gung mdzub sprad dang 'khyud//gtor ma sbyin 
87a5(rgyud): pa'i phyag rgya'o//thal mo khong stong shubs ltar gdon//bdug spos 'bul ba'i phyag rgya'o//lag gnyis so sor gung phrin bzhug/mtheb mnan bzhag brkyang me tog rgya//thal sbyar gung phreng mdzub mo bkug/mthe'u chung mtheb brkyang zhal zas rgya//
87a6(rgyud): skyil krung 'dug ste snyim pa zer//mthe bo gnyis ni nang du bkug/sor gshibs nas g.yab byed pa ni//dpa' bo gtso bo spyan drang rgya//thal sbyar mdzub bkug mthe bos mnan//byang chub sems tshogs 'gugs pa'i rgya//snyim bskyed mthe'u chung 
87a7(rgyud): gshibs te brkyang //lhag ma brgyad po so sor dgug/nyan thos rang rgyal rdzu 'phrul can//ma lus thams cad 'gugs pa'i rgya//khu tshur g.yas pa g.yon steng bzhag/mdzub gnyis brkyang ste so sor dgug/cung zad skyed par byed pa ni//pho nya pho mo 'gugs pa'i rgya//khu 
87b1(rgyud): tshur rgyab sprad zur mdzub ni//so sor drang por gshibs nye ba rkyang //ma shes gnyis chung zad skad de dgug/ba ri gnyen bshams 'gugs pa'i bu//g.yon min g.yas pas de ltar gyur//dbur zhugs drang srong 'gugs pa'i rgya//g.yon pa chu tshur gung mo bsgreng //dngos grub 
87b2(rgyud): sring mo 'gugs pa'i rgya//ma g.yas ba nad nyis btags kyang //mthe bos mdzub brtan pa bu mgo zhabs//mar du na ba sogs ma mo nam ma//las kyi dus su 'gugs par 'gyur//nges kyis bteg cing drang po yi//pus mo dag la rkang mthil sbyar//spyi bor me tog 
87b3(rgyud): thal sbyar yang //phyogs der g.yon cing 'bru tshugs pa lta//zlum pa'i stabs kyis 'di kun gyis//bgegs tshogs 'joms shing rnal 'byor bzung //lag pa g.yas nI gyen du brkyang //g.yon ba snying gar sdigs mdzub ste//rkang gnyis sprad nus ting brdebs//hUM 
87b4(rgyud): brjod drag skad mdun du lta//sa gsum g.yo byed zla gam stabs//rkang g.yon brkyang pa'i stabs dang ni//ha ha brjod cing zhabs rgyab cing //phan tshun bsnol nas bgegs la bsnun//'khor lo phyag rgya phye mar bya//rkang g.yas brkyang pa'i stabs dang ni//rdo rje thal mo 
87b5(rgyud): ma brdal bsnun//khro gnyer mig can mam chu mnan//yan lag gnyis gnyis me ltar sprad//rkang pa mnyam stabs hUM sgra can//lag gnyis sdigs mdzub phyag rgya'o//rkang gnyis mnyam du bzhag pa las//g.yas kyi g.yon bskor lte ba yin//
87b6(rgyud): mdun du g.yas te rgyab tu g.yon//gya tshom gsum gsum mnan pa ni//rtse gsum ya ni te rtse gsum rgya//lag pa steng brkyang dbyig bsgreng ltar//bgegs la bsnun zhing 'bru tshugs pa lta//g.yas su tho ba hUM brjod brdeg/g.yon pa snying ga'i sdigs 
87b7(rgyud): mdzub ste//gnyis ka bgrad pa 'khor lo bya//nub bltas rkang pa mnyam bzhag nas//mdun g.yas rgyab dang g.yon phyogs su//g.yon pas bgegs gsum mnan bya zhing //rim pas me dang ral sri dang //sdigs mdzub zhags pa phyogs bzhir bcing //ha hUM 
88a1(rgyud): @#//drag brjod brjid gnod rgya//rkang gnyis bus pa rgyud lag pa gnyis//bu mo brgyud gcig gis mnan//bgegs bskrad kyis ni bcu tshugs blta//cig cig rdo rje stabs su bcad//rkang pa g.yas g.yon ris kyis bskor//lho byang g.yon ti kar nu thag las//
88a2(rgyud): brgyad zhing rab tu brnam par bya//rgyal nas spang dang tho ba dang //rdo rje dang ni zhags pa'i rgya//phyogs bzhir bya ste 'khor lo'i stabs//phyag rgya dang ni stang stabs kyis//phyogs skyong rnams kyang skrag byed na//'byung po'i tshogs bsdoms smos ci dgos//thams cad 
88a3(rgyud): dbang du sdud par byed//de nas phyogs bcu bcing ba yi//dbang stabs khyad par bshad par bya//shar du rkang pa mnyam pa yis//rdo rje thal mo gyen du brdeg/lhor ni rkang rting sprad de gdang //hUM sgrogs thal mo mdun rgyab sbyar//nub tu rkang sprad lag pa gnyis//so sor sdig pa 
88a4(rgyud): mdzub gyen du brkyang //byang du ral gri'i phyag rgya dang //rkang pa la gcig sa la bsgreng //khu tshur mdzub sbyar dpral bar bzlog/rkang gnyis mnyam byas res kyis phyar//mtshe gtsigs hUM sgrogs khro gnyer blta//byang shar thig ler grags pa'i rgya//khu tshur mthe'u 
88a5(rgyud): chung mche ba'i rgya//rkang pa res bteg shar lhor phye//lho nub rkang gnyis res kyis brdab//zhags pas 'debs dang ral gris gcod//nub byang rkang gnyis brdab pa dang //lag gnyis mtheb sprad 'khor lo bskor//yang na tshe gcig phyag rgyas gdab//
88a6(rgyud): stang stabs thams cad ha hUM sgrogs//de nas rig sngags bzlog pa yi//phyag rgya cho ga bshad par bya//khu tshur gung mo brkyang rtse sprad//mdzub gnyis de yi 'gram du bcang //sngags dang phyag rgya thams cad kyis//lha klu srin po gnod sbyin dang //mkha' 'gro 
88a7(rgyud): 'byung po mi ma yin//gsod dang skrod de me ltar mdzad//'phags pa rdo rje khro bo'i rgyal po'i rtog pa bsdus pa'i rgyud las sngags dang phyag rgya'i le'u ste gsum pa'o////bla na med pa'i res ris sku//cho ga de nas bshad par bya//bris sku 'dod pas 
88b1(rgyud): thams cad du//dge ba'i ras bzangs btsal nas kyang //bzungs te sngags kyis bzung byas la//bud sgrig tu ni bskor bar bya//chung bzangs dal zhing dul ba g.yos//chos thos ri mo mkhas la sgrig/sred chung yid gzhungs khro med pas//sen rtsi shun kan 
88b2(rgyud): tshal sbyar//spong dang mu zi kan ltar ldang //pa bla kra ni bla na ser//yi khri sgo nga mtshal spags dmar//thams dang tsha phreng shun bru nir sngo//skyig dang mthu nag po ste//sha tA dag kyang dkar por 'gyur//sa snod pho ba tang lpags snyigs byug/pir 'jam 
88b3(rgyud): rab 'bring tha ma bri//rgyal dad sa ri nam gru dang //snon snubs srin drug lha mtshams shes//de nas yang dag rab bshad pa//thub pa'i dge ba ras ris la//dbus su byang chub shing mchog drung //gsang mdog 'khor lo sgyur ba'i chas//chos 'chad 
88b4(rgyud): phyag rgya ngur smrig ma nas la//logs su 'dab bzangs rgyal po gnyis//rnyim pa me tog bkang ba'i gzugs//g.yas su 'jig rten dbang po dkar//ngur smrig gyon pas thal mo sbyar//de yi zhabs su mi g.yo ba//spyan gsum khros pa 
88b5(rgyud): klu yis brgyan//na ra'i stod g.yogs stug smad dkris//rin chen rgyan can do ker can//rdob dmar po'i khri steng na//ral gri dang ni zhags pa dang //g.yon pa cung zad brkyang ste bzhengs//gtso bo'i g.yon gyi phyogs cha la//rdo rje 
88b6(rgyud): 'dzin sngo ngur smrig can//rdo rje dang ni zhags pa 'chang //de yi 'og tu sgrub po ni//pus btsugs spos kyi khang bu 'dzin//gtso bo'i zhabs su khro bo che//nag po khams gsum brtan pa yi//lag gnyis rin chen khri la bteg/gtso bo sna tshogs pad+ma ste//
88b7(rgyud): 'jig rten dbang dang khro bo yi//rdo rje 'dzin dang bsgrub pa'i sto//yan lag lnga pa sdong po gcig/klu yi bu mo mdzes ma gnyis//bang gis 'khyud de 'dzin par mdzad//zhi ba rgyas pa dbang gi phyir//de nas bris sku yang dag bshad//dbus su shAkya thub 
89a1(rgyud): @#//pa dang //g.yas su 'phags pa 'jam dpal te//g.yon du phyag na rdo rje'o//bsnyen pa'i don du ras ris ni//shin tu blta dka' gsus po che//zhal gcig phyag bzhi klu yis brgyan//kha nas khrag 'dzag rtse 'dril ba//mche gtsigs dri sgra 
89a2(rgyud): cher sgrogs pa//spyan gsum nyi ma shar ka'i 'od//dung sprig mnabs po cher sprin mdog/sku la thod rlon phreng ba can//dbu la thod pa tshar bzhis brgyan//g.yon du rin chen zur phud 'phyang //bskal pa'i me 'od 'bar ba yis//phyogs kyi bgegs 
89a3(rgyud): tshogs sreg par mdzad//g.yas kyi dang po rdo rje ste//gnyis pa sang ral gri 'od 'bar 'chang //g.yon gyi dang po rdo rje zhags//gnyis pa sdigs mdzub brgyad ste mdzad//rin chen rdo yi khri stengs su//bgegs kyi rgyal po mnan nas kyang //g.yon brkyang 
89a4(rgyud): pa yi gar gyis gnas//de yi mdun du zhi ba'i byad//spos khang bzung ba'i sgrub po bri//ci 'byor rgyun du mchod par bya//mngon spyod bsgrub pa'i ras ris ni//de nas yang dag bshad par bya//dur khrod ras la mi g.yo ba//mdog nag spyan gsum zur mig lta//
89a5(rgyud): dur smrig gos gyon khro gnyer ldan//ral gri dang ni zhags pa 'chang //bris nas bdag gi khrag gis ni//spyan phye gsol ba btab par bya//ri mo 'di ni rig 'dzin kyang //sgrub po mngon spyod smos ci dgos//yang na sna tshogs las kyi phyir//mthing ga na ra'i rgyan can 
89a6(rgyud): ni//brkyang bskum rdo rje dril bu 'dzin//pad+ma zla ba la bzhugs bri//dmag dpung rengs pa'i ras ris ni//gser mdog khro bo mi g.yo ba//mi sdug mche ba gtsigs pa'i sku//'bar phreng 'khrigs pa'i 'od ldan pa//zhal bzhi phyag kyang bzhi pa ste//ral gri 
89a7(rgyud): zhags pa dang por 'dzin//rin po che dang sdigs mdzub 'og/zhabs bzhi bdud bzhi snying gar ro//de nas rig 'dzin dngos grub kyi//ras ris bri ba yang dag bshad//mi g.yo khro bo zhal gcig pa//mdangs bzang zar ma'i me tog mdog/ral gri 
89b1(rgyud): zhags pa sdigs mdzub can//pus mo g.yas bteg g.yon pa ni//sa la zur zad btsugs par mdzad//mkha' la lha yi bu gnyis kyis//dbugs 'byin mdzad pa nyams dga'i gzugs//g.yas su rnam snang mdzad bri ste//g.yon du 'od dpag med pa'o//
89b2(rgyud): de bdun sgrub po nyid kyang bri//gang yang rig ma thams cad la//mchod de me tog gtor nas kyang //spyan phyin bza' gshol byas te//rten 'brel sngags kyis rab gnas bya//de rjes smon lam gdab pa ni//oM ldan bdag gis bsags pa yi//bsod nams 
89b3(rgyud): grangs med gzhal mi lang //de bzhin 'gro ba ma lus pa//bla med go 'phang thob par shog/sems can nga rgyal bdud 'gro dang //yi dgas gang dag grangs med rnams//bdag gi bsod nams mthu yis ni//thams cad bde bar ldan gyur cig/gang yang 
89b4(rgyud): lha mi dbul 'phongs dang //mi nang mu ge dgra chom sogs//bdag gi bsod namasamathu yis ni//de dag thams cad bral gyur shog/ji snyed sems can thams cad rnams//de snyed sdug bsngal rgya mtsho kun//sna tshogs de dag kun zhi nas//'ga' yang sdug 
89b5(rgyud): bsngal med par shog/sems can gang dag lus ngag yin//mi dge spyod pa bral gyur nas//dge ba phun tshogs spyod gyur te//kun kyang bde ldan thob gyur shog/de nas rgyal sras kun btud de//lag na dri dang me tog sogs//mchod pa'i yo byad 
89b6(rgyud): kun thogs nas//bla 'og khra dang gsal bya'i phyir//bde gshegs bsdu don 'di skad brjod//oM bde bar gshegs pa'i bcom ldan 'das//skyob pa dngos su bzhugs pa rnams//bdag la slar yang brtse ba'i phyir//rgyal ba sras bcas la mi mnga' la//
89b7(rgyud): bdag don grub par 'gro ba la//brtse phyir slar yang byon par mdzod//de nas gtor ma me tog dri//dkyil 'khor rgyan dang rdul tshon rnams//dngos grub dam tshig gnod spang phyir//lag pas res par mi bya ste//gos kyi phur ma la 
90a1(rgyud): sogs pa//ci bde bas ni phyogs nas kyang //skye 'gag med pa'i sngags brjod na//chu bo la ni dor bar bya//'bras dang 'o ma sha khar//mar dang bcas pa bskol byas te//'bras zan dang ni lhan cig tu//dga' ba skyed phyir du dge 'dun dang //
90a2(rgyud): bram ze la ni ston mo bya//de nas bris sku bstan par ni//gsang ba'i gnas su mchod par bya//khyad par tshes brgyad dus su mchod//bla ma la ni yon dbul zhing //rtag tu sngags 'dzin thams cad kyang //mnyes par bya zhing khros pa min//sems can 
90a3(rgyud): ma mos spang bar bya//dkon mchog gsum la nges bskyed de//the tshom yi lo spang bar bya'o//rang gi phung po bsnyen bkur phyir//khrus dang gtsang sgra ldan byas nas//gos sgra dri ma med pa bgo//zas kyang 'bras sogs dri med la//nyung ngu bza' 
90a4(rgyud): zhing btung bar bya//mangs pas gnyid dang le lo dang //dri rab ni bskyed par 'gyur//tho rengs dang por langs nas ni//srod thal bar du brtson par bya//nam gyi gung la gnyid pa'i tshe//'gro la phan bya'i sems kyis nyal//'phags pa rdo rje khro bo'i rgyal po'i 
90a5(rgyud): rtogs bsdus pa'i rgyud las bris sku'i le'u ste bzhi pa'o////de nas yang dag bshad bya ste//lha bsgom pa yi rim pa ni//rang gzugs stong par byas nas zhu//yi ge phreng ba'i 'khor lo las//sgra grags pa ni rig pa che//
90a6(rgyud): zla ba'i gzugs brnyan lta bur snang //der son pa ni rdo rje sogs//rang gi phyag rgya nyid yin no//de las gyur pa'i sku mchog ni//'ja' tshon shar ba ji lta bar//mtshan dpe mdangs 'od yang dag ldan//brjid cing dgyes pa'i mdangs dang ldan//sgeg 
90a7(rgyud): cing rol pa'i cha byad ni//byis pa gzhon nu'i lang tsho 'dra//rin chen rgyan dang na bza' ni//gzugs mdzes lha yi ci lta ba//khros pa'i srin po lta bu ste//skra dang kha spu la sogs pa//smra sogs ser zhing rtsub la 'khyil//lha 
90b1(rgyud): tshogs 'dus khrod rgyan rnams ni//grub cing blo ldan rab tu khros//na bza' rlon pa phrag can ni//steng gi byams su yod par mdzad//ha ha hUM hUM pa sha ra sha kyi//da skad 'phyug sgra spyugs blu ni//ri rab 'bum ba snyil cha la tsham ltar//lus rab ltos 'gros 
90b2(rgyud): brgyan pa ni//gzi ldan 'khyud cing phun sgra 'don//'phro 'du yod kyi dkyil 'khor ni//nyi 'bum dus mo'i bskal pa 'dra//rab gdug mtshon cha du ma ni//lha min dus kyi kha zug pa'i cha//khro bo khro mo'i tshogs 'byung ba//thams cad yi ge hUM sgra can//
90b3(rgyud): ma mo dang ni mkha' 'gro rnams//b+h+yo yig drag po'i bdag nyid can//thub pa yi ge hUM las bskyed//rgyal sras rig ma nyan thos dang //'jig rten skyong rnams sngags 'dis bskyed//na maHsarba ta thA ga te b+h+yaH/sa pa ri pU ra ke b+h+yaH/sarba thA/oM sarba saM ska 
90b4(rgyud): ra pa ri shud+d+he/d+harma te ga ga na sa mud ga te swa b+ha ba pa ri shud+d+he/ma hA na ya pa ri wA ri swA hA/rang gi lus la sems bcug nas//sdig pa thams cad rab tu zhi//ngang tshul bzang por yang dag byung //rig par gyur pa'i sems ldan nas//drang srong rdzu 'phrul 
90b5(rgyud): can du 'gyur//gzhan yang sdug bsngal zhi nus na//rang gi bde thob smos ci dgos//rang rang rigs kyi lha rnams kyi//gzugs brnyan phyag mtshan kha dog ni//dkyil 'khor ram ni ris ma las//gsung ba ji ltar bsgom par bya//de nas mi g.yo

96a3(rgyud): bya ba//phran tshegs las kyi sbyor ba gtso//gza' yi cho ga 'di yin te//gnod pa sel dang phan skyed mdzad//ba yi rnam ldas byugs pa yi//dkyil 'khor dbus su pad+ma ni//bdog sna tshogs nyis brtsegs kyi//dbus su sangs rgyas de bzhin stor//chos ni rdo rje chos dkar 
96a4(rgyud): po//shar du dge 'dun ljang gu'o//nub tu 'jig rten dbang phyug dmar//byang du phyag na rdo rje ste//byang shar du ni 'jam dpal ser//mir ni gshin rje'i gshed don nas//dbyug 'char ma he zhon pa'o//mi g.yo mgon po srin po phyogs//rta mgrin rlung gi phyogs su'o//
96a5(rgyud): dkon mchog gsum po chos 'chang rgya//sems dpa' khro bo rang rtags so//kun kyang rin chen khri la bzhugs//zhe rgyang rtsal gza' rnams te//shar du nyi ma dkar ser ni//rta ya zhon te pad+ma 'chang //nub tu zla ba dkar po ni//ku mu da 'chang dang ba zhon//lhor ni mig dmar kha dog 
96a6(rgyud): dmar//ril ba'i steng bzhugs me sgron bsnams//lag pa mdog ser ut+pal 'chang //byang du pad steng bzhugs pa yi//phur bu mdog sngon glegs bam thogs//shar lho phyogs su klu la bcibs//pa wa sangs dkar shing thog 'chang //lho nub gser gyi khri la bzhugs//byang shar 
96a7(rgyud): srin pa mdog nag ni//tshangs pa'i steng na dbyug pa thogs//nub byang sgra gcan dud ka ni//sbrul gyi steng na 'khor lo 'dzin//rnon po ljang gu ral gri 'chang //gtso bo'i drung du pad steng bzhugs//na maHsarba ta thA ga te b+h+yaH/sarba thA/oM b+ha gi nAM swA hA/
96b1(rgyud): hi ma'i snying po po//na maHsarba ta thA ga te b+h+yaHsarba pA pa ri pU ra ke b+h+yaHsarba thA/oM sarba tA yaM a swA hA/zla ba'i 'o//na maHsarba ta thA gA te b+h+yaH/sarbA pa ri pU ra keb+h+yaH/sarba thA/oM a b+hi tsa ya swA hA/snon bi'i 'o//na maHsarba ta thAg te b+h+yaH/sarbA pa ri bU ra ke b+h+yaHsarba thA /oM su d+hi shud+d+ha swA hA/yig dmar 
96b2(rgyud): gyi 'o//na maHsarba ta thA+A ga te b+h+yaHsarbA pa ri pU ra ke sh+yaHsarba thA/oM bi ru trA pa kA ya swA hA/gzar lhag gi'o//na maHsarba ta thA ka te b+h+yaH/sarbA pa ri b+hU ra ke b+h+yaH/sarbA thA/oM b+hi ka mya d+hA ya swA hA/phur bu'i 'o//na maHsarba ta thAng te b+h+yaHsarbA pa ri bU ra keb+h+yaHsarba thA/oM su d+hi shud+ha swA hA/pa pa 
96b3(rgyud): sangs kyi'o//na maHsarba ta thA gu te d+ya na sarbA pa ri pU ra ki h+yaHa thA/oM kr-i Sh+Na brtsi pha ka swA hA/sprin pa'i 'o//na maHsarba ta tha ga te b+h+yaH/sarbA pa ripU ra ke b+h+yaHsarba tha thA/oM bi kr-i ta la ku ru tsi swA hA/sgra ga man gyi'o//sngags pa'i brgyad stong brgyad brgya bzlas//Ni 'byor mchod de gsol ba 
96b4(rgyud): gdab//longs spyod spel cing 'jigs pa 'phel//nad med bgegs rigs 'joms par 'gyur//rims sogs gzas zin nad la yang //sku yi drung du gsol btab sel//zhi rgyas dbang drag gang sbyar ba//de dag thams cad 'grub par 'gyur//mi dang bya rgod sprul 
96b5(rgyud): rus pa//skabs su brtsams nas dgra khyim sku//dgra bo nad bskyed nor rnams brlag/rtag tu 'thab mo 'byung bar nges//stag spu thod rus 'ug pa'i spu//dgra bo'i skor sbas skrod par byed//ne'u le nag po wa sbal rus//rang khrag rtsangs pa'i mgo bo 
96b6(rgyud): rnams//gong du mi rab cas dgra skor sba//dgra bo 'khor bcas shi bar 'gyur//mi tshil dur thal rgya skyegs ni//thod pa so can nad blugs nas//gang gi sgor bzhag khang pa 'jig/ku sha'i gzugs brnyan rkang pas mnan//dbang sbyar 'dod pa'i 
96b7(rgyud): sngags bzlas na//rgyal po che yang dbang 'gyur na//'byung po gzhan lta smos ci dgos//bud med skyes phyugs la sogs la//rang ming ldan pa'i gzugs byas te//rkang pa g.yo nas nad ming bcas bzla//gang dang gang yang nges par 'grub//
97a1(rgyud): @#//sa la bgegs kyi gzugs bri ma te//sngags kyis brdungs na brlan par 'gyur//rim pas bgegs kyi gzugs byas te//gdan ci'i 'khrungs na sel bar nges//phur bu gdab skar mtshon chas brab//rdo rje ri rab cog brgyad ldan//sa 'khyam pa'i them par du//
97a2(rgyud): gzugs brnyan bcu gnyis dang bsam mnan//gzas zin nub mo ba'i maN+Dal//dri bzangs me tog sil ma bkram//pha rol phyin pa'i glegs bam ma bzhugs//zangs ma'i snod du mar bzhag ste//seng ldeng mes ni bskol bar bya//dngos grub rnam gsum 'grub pa dang //
97a3(rgyud): gnyer mthong bar yang 'di 'gyur ro//khros pas spyan gyi phyag rgya bya//hUM mtha' gsang sngags drag tu brjod//dur khrod thal ba gang byin pa//gdon gyis 'dzin nam bran du 'gyur//de nas mi g.yo spyan gcig pa'i//sngags kyi cho ga bshad par bya//ris ma'i drung du 'bum phrag gsum//
97a4(rgyud): brjod pas 'di yi bsnyen pa rdzogs//dben par zan gyi chang bu sbyin//des na rtag tu 'brel par 'gyur//mdzub mo'i rtse la hUM bkod nas//brjod pas sprin rnams 'dral bar byed//dgra bo lhar bcas gzugs brnyan la//rkang g.yon mnan bzlas lcags phur gdab//bka' 
97a5(rgyud): nyan 'gyur ro gtubs na shi//skud pa ser pos bcing nad dag/spang leb mnan na mnan par 'gyur//ra lpags brabs na bka' nyan 'gyur//srad bu yungs kar la bzlas te//'chang na gang 'dod srung bar byed//na maHsarba ta thA ga te b+h+yaHsarba bi shwa mu khe b+h+yoH/sarba thA/oM tsaN+Da 
97a6(rgyud): ma hA ro Sha Na hUM traM thA ma hA tsaN+Da/a tsa la ka na hUM hUM hUM a pra ti ha ta/ta thA ga ta dz+nyA sma ra hUM hUM phaT swA hA/rgya mtshor 'bab pa'i chu klung du//tsan+dan sbags pa'i pad+ma ni//sngags bcas ci tsam bsgyur ba'i grangs//gser gyi dong tse rnyed par 'gyur//bram ze 'am ni dge 'dun ras//
97a7(rgyud): rgyal po ngas sam gro ga la//pad+ma mu khyud gnyis ldan bri//lte bar oM hUM ming dang bcas//tsaN+Da ma hA ro Sha Na//phaT dang bcas pa 'dab mar bkod//mu khyud dang por rtsa sngags te//gnyis pa 'jam dpal sngags nyid bri//dri dang me tog sogs mchod 
97b1(rgyud): nas//skabs kyis dril nas lus la bcangs//gdon dang nad dang mu ge sel//g.yul du phyin na rgyal bar 'gyur//yang na ral gri sor bzhir bri//'od kyi ri mo nang chud pa//ye d+harmA la sogs kyis bskor//dbus su ming dang bcas pa yi//snying po sngags ni 
97b2(rgyud): khrigs su bri//'dis kyang sngon gyi yon tan 'gyur//mtho lngas 'khor ba'i bar bu ni//mtho gang 'thad du yod par bya//nang du mtshal gyis 'jam dpal sngags//mgo ni gyen du bltas pa bri//kha la nye bar rten 'brel sngags//spra tshil las byas dgra yi 
97b3(rgyud): gzugs//dug dang gri khrag gis bris nas//ming yig bsod par bcas pa ni//ling ga'i snying gar bcug byas te//dkyil 'khor gru gsum nag po yi//dbus yu mtho gang bus byas te//nang bzhag steng du bar bu sbub//ci srid mar gyi tshes gcig nas//
97b4(rgyud): zla ba rtsi snang zhag bar du//nyin mo rten 'brel sngags bzlas te//mtshan dus khro bo spyan gcig pa'i//sngags ni drag tu brjod par bya//mtshams dang ji srid mtshams bar du//g.yon du bskor zhing rtsa ba'i sngags//drug cu rtsa bzhi'i grangs su brjod//'jam 
97b5(rgyud): dpal sngags kyis chos gos srung //bde gshegs yi ge brgya pas ni//mchod rten la ni gseg shar ba sel//sangs rgyas bstan pa 'jig pa dang //sngags la 'phya zhing sems can 'tshe//log lta log par spyod byed cing //sems can la 'tshe byed pa ni//
97b6(rgyud): rgyal po khro bo che bcu dang //'jig rten skyong dang rgyal chen bzhi//mchog sa gza' dang rgyu gar tshogs//bstan dga' rnams kyis myur du sgrol//rgyud gzhan dag la grags pa yi//bden pa'i mthu yang du ma brjod//dur khrod sa la mchod rten ni//nag po 
97b7(rgyud): lcags su ma zhu gang ba'i//srog shing nag po gru gsum la//bde gshegs yi ge brgyabs dkri//gur ku ma yi khris bris pa'o//mya ngan med shing gru gsum la//rgya gram dbang chen rim par bca'//la~M dang sta~M b+ha ya nan brgyad//phyogs brgyad bri ba'i 
98a1(rgyud): @#//
98b1(rgyud): ni//mtshon gyis bsad pa'i thod pa la//sngags dang yul lha'i ming spel bri//lha yi gnas bcug de steng du//sngags bzlas phur pa snying ga bdag/gdug pa'i tsher ma'i hom sbar te//mi rta ba lang khyi ma he//sna tshogs khrag bsres 'di skad brjod//lha khyod yul 'dir thog ser 
98b2(rgyud): por//mid na bskal par khyod nyid sreg/de rjes dkar dang mngar mchod do//'phags pa rdo rje khro bo'i rgyal po chen po'i rtog pa bsdus pa'i rgyud las sbyor ba phran tshegs kyi le'u ste brgyad pa'o////de nas sbyin sreg las rnams kun//myur 'grub thabs ni bshad par bya//mngar gsum 
98b3(rgyud): rlung ba bsregs pa yis//gnod rigs thams cad sel bar byed//yam shing 'bras bu gsum bsregs shing //gdon nad thams cad zhi bar byed//ro ma can gyi lam shing la//'o ma dur ba til dang 'bras//snying rje'i sems kyis brgya bsregs na//gnod pa thams cad zhi 
98b4(rgyud): bar 'gyur//ga pur mngar gsum 'bru thams cad//dur ba dkar po'i yam shing rnams//'o ma can gyi shing la bsreg/nad dang gdon rnams thams cad ni//'o ma sbyin sreg brgya rtsa brgyad//byugs ngas chen po zhi bar 'gyur//dur ba mngar gsum ming bcas pas//bsregs 
98b5(rgyud): pas rta phyugs nad zhi byed//shar lho nub byang phyogs kyi ni//dkar nag dmar ser kha dog 'grub//khru gang khru gnyis tshad kyi thab//sor bzhi kha khyer rim pas btang //sems dpa' dang pad+ma'i rdo rje dang //gcig pus zhi rgyas dbang drag 'grub//de nas rgyas pa'i sbyin sreg ni//
98b6(rgyud): ci 'dod thob pa bshad par bya//pad+ma'i chu la 'bum phrag gcig/rgyal po 'thob ste blon po ni//ku mu da la 'bum phrag gsum//zho dang chang ra gsum dang bcas pa'o//a pa marga'i 'bras bu dang //mngar gsum 'bum gyis yul bdag 'thob//rigs bzang 
98b7(rgyud): 'bum phrag bdun gyis ni//yid la ci 'dod thob par 'gyur//dur ba til dang 'bras sogs bsreg/bud med bu bzang thob par 'gyur//nor 'dod pas ni rkun po yi//lag pa g.yas pa mdzod bzhag nas//mchod cing gsol ba btab pas ni//nor 
99a1(rgyud): @#//rnams myur du thob par 'gyur//yang na rgya yi pags pa la//gur gum gi'u wang yung bas ni//byi ba cher brjid ser po bri//shar na mdzad phug la bltas par bzhag zas kyi lhag mas mchod bya zhing //gsol ba btab pas nor rnyed 'gyur//bzhi mi bka'i 
99a2(rgyud): ka//bri yang ku ni nor rnyed do//'bras bsregs pas kyang nor chen 'thob//ku mu da yi 'bras sogs bsregs//longs spyod ci dang sa gzhi 'thob//bslab dang sprad rtsi mas//sbyin sreg 'bum gyis mchog stsol pa//gsum pa dag kyang mthong bar 'gyur//zhil po'i 'bras bu 
99a3(rgyud): mngar gsum bcas//'bum phrag gsum du bsregs pas ni//rang nyid rgyal po chen por 'gyur//mar dang yungs kar dkar pos ni//nim pa'i shing la 'bum bsregs na//snying po rnying pa rgyas par 'gyur//nas la sogs pa 'bru rigs rnams//gang bsregs gsol btab de rgyas 
99a4(rgyud): 'gyur//gang 'dod gos kyi kha dog can//me tog bsregs na gos rnyed 'gyur//spros shing bsregs na 'khor g.yog 'phel//zhwa mar seng ldeng me la stong //bsregs na nor rnams rnyed par 'gyur//de nas dbang gi bsregs lugs bshad//til dang 'bras sogs phub 
99a5(rgyud): ma bsregs//bud med dbang du byed pa'o//bri yang ku yi sbyin sreg gis//byis pa rnams ni dbang du 'gyur//'o ma sprad rtsi a ru ra yi//shing la bsregs na lha dbang byed//gsum po de dang gu gul bsregs//gnod sbyin rnams ni dbang du 'gyur//mya ngan med pa'i yam 
99a6(rgyud): shing gis//gnod sbyin mo rnams dbang du 'gyur//rgyal po'i yungs kar yungs mar bcas//bsregs pa sa rgyal po dbang du 'gyur//de nas drag po'i las grub pa'i//sbyin sreg yang dag bshad par bya//ming bcas tshad dang nim pa bsregs//bstan sdang dgra bo 
99a7(rgyud): myur du 'chi//dgra ming dug gis nim pa la//bris te dug dang lhan cig tu//sbrul shun thum po lnga byas nas//thod pa lnga yi nang bzhag ste//lnga po yungs mar gyis bkang nas//gsum ni sgyed bu gnyis brtsegs la//khros pas gcig tu skra grol bas//
99b1(rgyud): sngags brjod thal bar gyur bar du//nyin phyed bsregs na dgra 'chi nges//'chi ltas 'dab ma rtsa ba mun//ra khrag yungs mar tshwa chu skyegs//tsha ba'i bud shing yam shing la//bdun phrag ming dang bcas pa bsregs//stong phrag gnyis kyis rtag phyugs 'chi//bzhis ni bu 
99b2(rgyud): dang chung ma 'chi//dug gis nye du gnyen 'dun brlag/brgyad kyis dgra yi'ang srog 'chad//gal te skyabs su 'od gyur na//'o ma stong bsregs rnal du songs//khrag dang dug phye nim pa'i 'bras//yungs mar la sbags brgya rtsa brgyad//myos byed la bsregs dgra bo 'chi//yang 
99b3(rgyud): na rus phye nim pa'i lnga//rang gi khrag gam yungs mar sbag/yang na ske tshe dug dang ni//nim pa'i 'bras bus gsad par bya//yang na ni khrag gis bsregs pas//byang ma myur du gsod par 'gyur//na ni pa'i lnga dang ra khrag dug/dri ma lnga bcas gzugs snying gar//ming yig 
99b4(rgyud): bcug nas phur bus gdab//rig sngags byad ma nyid ldog 'gyur//rgya skyegs mi rus phye ma yis//mi lpags rlon la dgra gzugs ni//dug dang ske tshe cung zad byugs//nim pa'i me la khros pas bso//bsregs pas tsha ba'i rims kyis 'chi//dUr ba 'o mas gos par 
99b5(rgyud): 'gyur//dug dang nim pa sgog skya dang //mi rus phye bsregs phyugs rnams brlag/tsha gsum lan tshwa dUr rang skyur//yungs mar ngo sprel shun dang //zhim pa'i lnga dang mi rus phye//skye tshe dug phye sgog 'tshong dang //sgog skya tsher ma'i me la bsregs//dgra bo khrag tu 
99b6(rgyud): skyug ste 'chi//rgya skyegs lan tshwa ba lang mkhris//bsregs na mchin gas nad kyis 'chi//sge'u gsher lan tshwa tsha ba gsum//gzugs brnyan dur khrod mgal du ma la//mgo nas rkang pa'i bar du bsregs//thig nag gi sa ni gsod par 'gyur//
99b7(rgyud): 'o ma can gyi yam shing la//dkar gsum bsregs na gos par 'gyur//dri chus spangs pa'i sa las ni//thab khung byas te dug gis byugs//nim pa'i 'dab mar dag po'i ming //ra khrag gis bris rgyus pas dkris//tsha gsum shing gi mer 
100a1(rgyud): @#//bsregs pas//rgyal po myur du 'chi bar nges//dug khrag yan tshwa tsha ba gsum//ming bcas gzugs brnyan nim pa'i mer//bsregs na dgra bod mu rdzing ngam//mdze nad kyis ni gsod par 'gyur//tsha gsum ni ma pa dug dang dUr//khrag dang 
100a2(rgyud): lan tshwa'i gzugs brnyan ni//lan tshwa nim pa tsha ba'i mar//byugs nas arga'i shing la bsregs//mdze nad chen pos bzung nas 'chi//ra khrag tsha gsum yungs mar spos//nim pa'i lo man le sham//yungs mar dug bsregs tsha bas 'chi//na le sham 
100a3(rgyud): ma ma dug dang tsha//d+ha du ra lnga tsha ba gsum//sol ba la ni dgra gzugs bya//yungs mar byugs te bsregs na 'chi//tsha gsum pa yi 'bras su yis//nim pa'i 'ad pa mad gra mong bri//thod par dug yungs lhan cig bzhag/d+ha du ra lnga btsos 
100a4(rgyud): nas 'ching //rig ma byang du bltas nas kyang //rang nyid lho phyogs blta bar bya//tshangs pa la sogs lha tshogs dang //'jig rten skyong ba rdzu 'phrul can//ma mo mkha' 'gro sha za sogs//sa bcu'i dbang phyug byang chub sems//rig 'dzin khro bo'i rgyal po 
100a5(rgyud): dang //sangs rgyas dkyil 'khor lha rnams gson//sdig can nga rgyal gdug cing khro//chad lta sngags 'bya dkon mchog bcom//bstan la sngar zhing sems can 'tshe//mtshams med can 'di bsgral du gsol//rlung gis 'dar zhing char gyis gsher//
100a6(rgyud): mes gdung rdul tshub gam 'dir skye//seng ge mkha' lding stag khro bos//bcom zhing 'du srang bud rgod kyis//khrag 'jib bya tshogs mkha' la khyer//mngon spyod las kyi bsam gtan no//de nas sna tshogs las bshad pa//mi rus phye ma tshe skye tshe//gnod sbyin 
100a7(rgyud): 'khrig pa dug shing pa//tha dad ming bcas bsregs pas ni//bskrad dang dbye dang smyo bar byed//dUr ba 'o mar bcas pas sos//d+ha du ra yan lag lnga 'bras bu//tsher ma'i mes bsregs smyo bar byed//tsha gsum lan tshwas cha rims bskyed//yang na shing skam 
100b1(rgyud): lta bur skams//dzam pa'i ra dang rang sgyur bcas//dgra bo grang ba'i rims kyis 'debs//kha'i lho e ra ta'i mar khus//byugs pa sna dur me la bsregs//la galto bas kha ta lta bur ni//dgra bos mtha' kun tu 'khyam//ske tshe mi rus lan tsha dug/yungs mar nim 
100b2(rgyud): pa'i me la bsregs//dgra bo rtag tu 'thab pa dang //nad rnams kun gyis 'debs par 'gyur//dur khrod thal kha rol bar bcas//dag po'i gzugs byas dum bur gnon//tsha ma ngam bur bur 'debs pa ni//khol mig nad ngan skyed par byed//bya rgod 'ug pa 
100b3(rgyud): byi la sha//ne'u le'i spu rnams khyer ba'i mer//drug cu rtsa brgyad bsregs pa yis//thun gnyis myur du 'byed par reg/khrag can ras la gzugs bris na//dang g.yon sker cha dan tsham ngam bcas//'dod bcas sngags ni drag po yis//brgyal dang rims nad 
100b4(rgyud): mtsher nad bskyed//dgra yi skra dang rkang rjes sa//dgra gzugs dug dang rke tshes byugs//tsher mes bsregs na rengs par 'gyur//dgra yi pha rus dug rang skyur//gzugs byad nim pa'i mer bsregs na//dgra bo gdon chen nad kyis 'debs//phag rtug 
100b5(rgyud): sU ha arga'i shing //tsher mes bsregs nam go 'khor 'gyur//ne'u le byi la byi tsher spu//bsregs na bud med bu brlag 'gyur//mi dang ne'u le khwa rus pa//yungs mar nim pa'i me la bsregs//grong dang grong khyer skrod par byed//stag spu thod rus 'ug pa'i 
100b6(rgyud): sa//sa ha me tog na le sham//nim pa'i lo ma dug bsregs byed//nim pa'i 'bras bu dug lan tshwa//d+ha dur 'bru bsregs nad skyed byed//dug khrag d+ha dur 'bras bu yis//nim pa'i shing la dgra ming bri//tsha gsum lan tshwa khrag gis byugs//lcags snod 
100b7(rgyud): yungs mar nang bzhag nas//nim pa'i shing gi me yis btso//dgra bo lha dang 'byed par 'gyur//mi khyi bya rgod rus phye dug/ske tshe yungs mar sbrul shun dang //nim pa'i lnga dang skags lan tshwa//tsher mes bsregs na nad kyis 'debs//ske tshe dug 
101a1(rgyud): @#//skeg ma sprul shun tshang ba'i mar//gzugs byas tsha ba'i mi la gcung //char ches sa dang thog 'thab 'gyur//'phags pa rdo rje khro bo'i rgyal po'i rtog pa bsdus pa'i rgyud las sbyin sreg gi le'u ste dgu pa'o////
101b1(rgyud): do//e ma sangs rgyas bsam mi khyab//dam pa'i chos kyang bsam mi khyab//'gro la phan dang don byed pa'i//cho ga'i ming dang shes rab dang //snying rje'i dbye bas rgyas gyur nas//mnar med la sogs ngan 'gro yi//'jigs rnams las brgal ba da ltar//rnal 'byor can de mdzes par 
101b2(rgyud): spyod//de nas mthu stobs cher ldan pa'i//phyogs bcu'i khro bo kun 'dus nas//shin tu rol pas lhan tshig gis//ched du brjod pa 'di brjod do//e ma sangs rgyas chos chen dang //dge 'dun dkon mchog bsam mi khyab//'khor ba'i mun pa chen por ni//bying bas blta dka' rtogs pa yis//
101b3(rgyud): sngags dang phyag rgya bsam mi khyab//mthong dang thos pa tsam gyis kyang //blta dkar 'byung po drag po rnams//lhag pa'i sems ni skyes par 'gyur//de nas rig pa'i lha mo che//spyan la sogs pa rgya chen rnams//ngo mtshar gyur nas spyan gdangs te//snyan pas ched du 'di brjod do//
101b4(rgyud): ye ma sangs rgyas rmad du byung //e ma sangs rgyas dpa' bo che//e ma sangs rgyas rab tu zab//chos rnams kyi med 'gag pa med//sgyu 'phrul sna tshogs rol pa ltar//bla ma gdul bya tsam mdzad pa//spros pa dang bral spros pa bral//sku ma rab tu 'bil bar mdzad//de nas phyogs bcu'i sangs 
101b5(rgyud): rgyas rnams//stong gsum mi mjed 'jig rten khams//gdul ba dag la rab gzigs nas//'dul mdzad rdo rje 'chang ba la//ngo mtshar rmad kyi gsung gis ni//ched du brjod pa 'dis bstod do//oM zhes dang po phyag 'tshal bkra shis ltar byed pa//ser sna rnam par dag nas yo byad dbang thob

106b3(rgyud): 'khor mdzes gyur pa//ka la ping ka'i skad kyis sdang ba 'dul//mda' dang ru gzhu stobs chen mtshon cha bsnams//stobs kyi lha ste stobs dang brtson pas 'dul//rgyan rnams sna tshogs legs par smras pa ste//rgyal ba rig sngags 'chang ba mang pos bstod//
106b4(rgyud): tshogs mchod dga' byed rnams kyis rab bstod pa//chu srin 'dzin khri'i 'jigs las rab grol mdzad//gzugs med mang ba zhing bde spyi dang ma sbyin pa//lha yul bgro ba'i khyad par dkyil 'khor can//mtshungs med pas kyang mtshungs med mthu rtsal mang ba//
106b5(rgyud): stabs gshegs yon tan che mchog 'od zer ldan//'jig rten nang na mkhas pa spyan ras gzigs//kha gzhi'i dkyil 'khor dbus kyi dkyil 'khor can//'od zer mang po stong phrag zad mi shes//nyon mongs rdul rnams thams cad sel mdzad pa//mtha' dang 
106b6(rgyud): bral ba kun tu gzigs pa ste//rab zhi kun las rab bab pa'i spyod yul can//don dang tshul la sems can gcig dpa' ba//theg pa bzang po 'dzin pa'i nyi ma lags//rdul dang sbyin dang tshul la dad pa brnyes//skye dgu rnams kyi dri ma ma lus bcom//
106b7(rgyud): blo gros bzang po rab dgyes ston pa po//bde ldan gos brgyan yang ba sna tshogs can//bcom ldan 'byung ba 'jigs pa med par mdzad//rtag tu dge ba kun mchog sbyin pa po//rgyal bas gsungs pa'i rnam thar brnyes pa'i blo//bde

107a2(rgyud): mtshungs shing chos mchog rin chen mtshungs//dge 'dun mtshungs shing chags bral thub par dka'/khyad par gsum 'phags rin chen mchog chen mtshungs//khyab bdag nyon mongs rnam gsum rdul bsal ba//rdo rje sems mchog khyod la phyag 'tshal lo//

107a4(rgyud): la bstod pa yi//ming brgya rtsa brgyad skyon sel legs pa mnga'//ji ste la la rtag tu dge mchog 'dod//spyan ras gzigs dbang la zhi stod byed pa//des ni ring por mi thogs yon tan gyi//longs spyod mchog chen chos ni thol bar 'gyur//
